- **gdd-brief**: Fixes what has to pass for one change in a Gate-Driven Development (GDD) project before anyone builds it, with a first estimate of its risk. - **gdd-budget**: Forecasts GDD design-cycle spend before the bill arrives. Reads .design/telemetry/costs.jsonl (cost per cycle) + .design/budget.json (the project_cap), runs the pure cost-forecast model via agents/cost-forecaster.md, and projects the next N cycles - surfacing 'at the current rate you'll hit your $X project cap in Y cycles.' Supports --scenario best|typical|worst and --cycles N. Read-only - it forecasts and warns; it never spends, edits budget.json, or halts (the budget-enforcer hook halts). - **gdd-build**: Builds one change through its gates in a Gate-Driven Development (GDD) project, from its brief and its sealed checks. - **gdd-cache-manager**: Maintains .design/cache-manifest.json for Layer B explicit cache per D-08. Computes deterministic SHA-256 input-hash from (agent-path + sorted-input-file-paths + input-content-hashes). On spawn: lookup key → return cached blob if within TTL, else miss. On completion: write result + TTL. Consulted by hooks/budget-enforcer.ts before every Agent spawn. ([hegemonart/get-design-done](https://github.com/hegemonart/get-design-done/tree/HEAD/skills/cache-manager) · ★3 · 2 copies)

- **gdd-roi**: Shows whether GDD spend actually shipped anything. 'Shipped' = a commit surviving &gt;= the window (default 14 days), which catches revert-after-bug-discovery. Markdown table, not a GUI. Read-only - it reads git log + cost telemetry and reports. Use to see which cycles were worth their spend. ([hegemonart/get-design-done](https://github.com/hegemonart/get-design-done/tree/HEAD/skills/roi) · ★3 · 2 copies)
